{
  "description": "Create Helm chart for all envs",
  "subagent_type": "kubernetes-specialist",
  "prompt": "Create the complete Helm chart for the IQ Portal workload across 3 environments. The chart directory exists under each environment at environments/{env}/helm/kix-app-iqportal/.\n\nIMPORTANT: First read ALL template files from the reference repo at /tmp/kix-services-backstage/environments/dev/helm/backstage/ to understand the exact patterns. Then adapt for IQ Portal.\n\nKey differences from Backstage:\n1. App port is 8080 (not 7007)\n2. Health check paths: /health (liveness), /api/health (readiness)\n3. No configmap needed (IQ Portal uses env vars, not app-config.yaml)\n4. Cloud SQL proxy does NOT use --auto-iam-authn (password auth, not IAM)\n5. More env vars from secrets (DB_PASSWORD, JWT_SECRET, etc.)\n6. No command/args needed (uses Dockerfile ENTRYPOINT)\n7. Container runs as user 1001 (not 1000)\n\n## Files to create in environments/dev/helm/kix-app-iqportal/:\n\n### Chart.yaml\n```yaml\napiVersion: v2\nname: kix-app-iqportal\ndescription: Konecta IQ Portal - AI-powered knowledge platform and agent marketplace\ntype: application\nversion: 1.0.0\nappVersion: \"0.9.1\"\nkeywords:\n  - iq-portal\n  - ai-platform\n  - express\n  - nuxt\nmaintainers:\n  - name: KIX Platform Team\n    email: platform-team@konecta.com\nhome: https://docs.kix-platform.konecta.com/services/iq-portal\nsources:\n  - https://github.com/konecta-ix-applications/kix-apps-konectaiq\n```\n\n### values.yaml\nCreate base values matching the Backstage pattern but adapted for IQ Portal. Read /tmp/kix-services-backstage/environments/dev/helm/backstage/values.yaml for structure. Include sections for:\n- image (repository, tag, pullPolicy)\n- replicaCount: 1\n- service (type: ClusterIP, port: 80, targetPort: 8080)\n- serviceAccount (create, name, annotations)\n- database (cloudSqlProxy config: image, instanceConnectionName, port)\n- env (plain env vars from config)\n- secrets (secretRef names for db-credentials, app-secrets, integration-secrets, storage-config)\n- resources (requests: cpu 250m, memory 256Mi; limits: cpu 1000m, memory 512Mi)\n- autoscaling (enabled: false, min: 1, max: 5, targetCPU: 70, targetMemory: 80)\n- podDisruptionBudget (enabled: false, minAvailable: 1)\n- networkPolicies (comprehensive config matching Backstage pattern with all 10 policy types)\n- commonLabels\n- podAnnotations, podLabels\n- securityContext (runAsNonRoot: true, runAsUser: 1001, fsGroup: 1001, seccompProfile: RuntimeDefault)\n- containerSecurityContext (allowPrivilegeEscalation: false, readOnlyRootFilesystem: true, capabilities.drop: [ALL])\n- probes (liveness: /health:8080, readiness: /api/health:8080, startup: /health:8080)\n- gateway (enabled: false, className, hostname, certificateMap)\n- iap (enabled: false, clientID, secretName)\n- securityPolicy: \"\"\n\n### templates/_helpers.tpl\nMirror from Backstage but change all \"backstage\" references to \"kix-app-iqportal\". Include helpers for:\n- kix-app-iqportal.name\n- kix-app-iqportal.fullname\n- kix-app-iqportal.chart\n- kix-app-iqportal.labels\n- kix-app-iqportal.selectorLabels\n- kix-app-iqportal.serviceAccountName\n\n### templates/deployment.yaml\nMirror Backstage pattern but:\n- Port 8080 instead of 7007\n- NO command/args (uses Dockerfile ENTRYPOINT)\n- NO configmap volume mount\n- ADD /tmp emptyDir volume mount (for temp files)\n- Environment variables from multiple secret refs:\n  - DB_USER and DB_PASSWORD from kix-app-iqportal-db-credentials\n  - JWT_SECRET from kix-app-iqportal-app-secrets\n  - GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID from kix-app-iqportal-app-secrets\n  - CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_ID, CHANNELHUB_CLIENT_SECRET, CHANNELHUB_CONNECTION_ID from kix-app-iqportal-integration-secrets\n  - RAG_PIPELINE_TOKEN from kix-app-iqportal-integration-secrets\n  - GCS_IMAGE_BUCKET, GCS_VIDEO_BUCKET, GCS_RAG_BUCKET from kix-app-iqportal-storage-config\n- Plain env vars from values.env (PORT, NODE_ENV, DB_HOST=localhost, DB_PORT=5432, DB_NAME, GCP_PROJECT_ID, VERTEX_AI_*, RAG_*, CLOUD_TASKS_*, etc.)\n- Cloud SQL Proxy sidecar WITHOUT --auto-iam-authn\n- runAsUser: 1001 (not 1000)\n- Probes: liveness /health, readiness /api/health, startup /health\n\n### templates/service.yaml\nClusterIP service, port 80 → targetPort 8080\n\n### templates/serviceaccount.yaml\nConditional creation with Workload Identity annotation\n\n### templates/hpa.yaml\nHorizontalPodAutoscaler v2, conditional on autoscaling.enabled\n\n### templates/pdb.yaml\nPodDisruptionBudget, conditional on podDisruptionBudget.enabled\n\n### templates/networkpolicy.yaml\nMirror all 10 network policies from Backstage exactly (read the reference file):\n1. Default deny all ingress\n2. Allow GCP health check IPs (35.191.0.0/16, 130.211.0.0/22)\n3. Allow from kube-system\n4. Allow GMP collectors (conditional)\n5. Custom ingress rules\n6. Allow DNS egress\n7. Allow Google APIs egress\n8. Allow internet egress (conditional)\n9. Custom egress rules\n10. Allow same-namespace\n\n### templates/gateway.yaml\nGKE Gateway with HTTP + HTTPS listeners, conditional on gateway.enabled\n\n### templates/httproute.yaml\nHTTPRoute for HTTPS traffic to service\n\n### templates/httproute-redirect.yaml\nHTTPRoute for HTTP→HTTPS redirect\n\n### templates/gcpbackendpolicy.yaml\nGCPBackendPolicy with IAP, Cloud Armor, logging, conditional on gateway.enabled && iap.enabled\n\n### templates/healthcheckpolicy.yaml\nHealthCheckPolicy targeting the service, health path /health port 8080\n\n### templates/NOTES.txt\nPost-install instructions adapted for IQ Portal\n\n## AFTER creating all files in dev:\nCopy the ENTIRE helm directory to qa and prod:\n- environments/qa/helm/kix-app-iqportal/ (identical templates and Chart.yaml and values.yaml)\n- environments/prod/helm/kix-app-iqportal/ (identical templates and Chart.yaml and values.yaml)\n\nNote: values-{env}.yaml files will be created later by the generate script. For now, create placeholder files:\n- environments/dev/helm/kix-app-iqportal/values-dev.yaml (empty placeholder with comment \"# Auto-generated - do not edit manually\")\n- environments/qa/helm/kix-app-iqportal/values-qa.yaml (same)\n- environments/prod/helm/kix-app-iqportal/values-prod.yaml (same)\n\nCreate all necessary parent directories before writing files.",

    42→# --- Generate YAML from config.json ---

   164→          database: ${db_name}
   165→          ssl: false
   166→
   167→$(if [[ "$auth_guest_enabled" == "true" ]]; then

   193→elif [[ -n "$github_host" ]]; then

   202→

```json
{
  "command": "git commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nfeat: add complete IQ Portal workload infrastructure\n\nAdd Terraform, Helm charts, ArgoCD manifests, and CI/CD workflows\nfor deploying the KonectaIQ Portal to GKE across dev, qa, and prod\nenvironments, following the kix-services-backstage reference pattern.\n\nKey components:\n- Environment-based structure with config.json as SSOT\n- Terraform: namespace, IAM (cross-project), Cloud SQL DB/user,\n  ExternalSecrets, Certificate Manager, Workload Identity\n- Helm: deployment with Cloud SQL proxy sidecar (password auth),\n  GKE Gateway API, IAP, network policies, HPA, PDB\n- ArgoCD: per-environment Application manifests\n- GitHub Actions: validate-and-plan, validate-plan-apply,\n  argocd-deploy, manual-validate-plan workflows\n- generate-helm-values.sh: auto-generates values from config.json\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.6 <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Commit all infrastructure files"
}
```
